# Dokan New Dashboards — E2E Test Case Checklist

> **Scope:** New **Admin** dashboard (`wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard`) and new **Vendor** dashboard (`/dashboard/new/`). **Editions:** Lite + Pro.
> **Legend:** `[x]` already automated · `[ ]` to add · ✅ covered · 🟡 partial (e.g. route-mount smoke only) · **[Priority·type]** · tags `@lite/@pro @admin/@vendor/@customer`.
> Types: happy, edge, negative, security, a11y, perf, regression. Priority: P1 critical · P2 important · P3 nice-to-have.

## Coverage summary

| Dashboard | Total cases | ✅ automated | 🟡 partial | ☐ to add |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Admin (`page=dokan-dashboard`) | 754 | 13 | 82 | 659 |
| Vendor (`/dashboard/new/`) | 552 | 116 | 63 | 373 |
| **Total** | **1306** | **129** | **145** | **1032** |


## A) Admin Dashboard — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard`

### Dashboard home — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/` _(mixed)_

The admin Dashboard home (route '/', id 'dashboard') mounts the React AdminDashboard on #dokan-admin-dashboard inside the WP admin shell. The page renders AdminHeader plus a fixed stack of sections in this order: TodoSection, AnalyticsSection, MonthlyOverviewSection (MonthPicker with left comparison ranges), SalesChartSection (D3 line chart with its own MonthPicker), VendorMetricsSection (Pro-only, gated by dokanAdminDashboardSettings.header_info.is_pro_exists), AllTimeStatsSection (formats total_sales/total_commissions via formatPrice), TopPerformingVendorsSection (DataViews table, own MonthPicker, padded to 5 rows of '--'), then a 2-column grid of MostReviewedProductsSection (DataViews) and MostReportedVendorsSection (DataViews, gated by dokanAdminDashboardSettings.show_most_reported_vendors). Every data section uses useDashboardApiData -> per-section skeleton while loading, a red error box (each with a section-specific message) on failure, and content otherwise. Data comes from dokan/v1/admin/dashboard/* REST endpoints (todo, analytics, monthly-overview, vendor-metrics, all-time-stats, sales-chart, top-performing-vendors, most-reviewed-products, most-reported-vendors). AdminNotices (separate component, fed by dokan/v1/admin/notices/admin) renders a slider with prev/next, an 'X of Y' counter, auto-slide that pauses on hover, action buttons/links, and a DokanModal confirmation when an action has confirm_message; it returns null on loading/error/empty. The MonthlyOverview/SalesChart/TopVendors MonthPickers support year navigation, are deselectable (clearing resets to the current month and refetches), and the date is sent as YYYY-MM. MonthlyOverview computes percentage change + up/down/neutral trend (with negative metrics like refund/abuse_reports/order_cancellation_rate inverted). Most* and TopVendors tables filter empty rows and pad to 5 with '--', use formatPrice for currency columns, and ShortContent/Tooltip for long text. Existing coverage: admin.spec.ts 'Admin Dokan Dashboard (React)' TC1 is a root-route mount smoke test only; legacy TC3 (adminDashboardRenderProperly) asserts section heading visibility, and TC4/TC5 cover promo/admin notices using a mocked endpoint. Section-level content, MonthPicker filtering, trend math, currency formatting, DataView padding, error/empty states, the notice slider/modal, Pro/Lite gating, and authorization are otherwise untested.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts`

### Vendors list — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors` _(mixed)_

VendorsPage (src/admin/dashboard/pages/vendors.tsx, route id 'vendors', PHP page includes/Admin/Dashboard/Pages/Vendors.php) renders an AdminDataViews (DataViews) table fed by GET /dokan/v1/stores with parse:false to read X-WP-Total and X-Status-All/Approved/Pending headers. Header shows title 'Vendors' + an 'Add Vendor' button (navigates /vendors/create) wrapped by two Slot fill points (before/after add-vendor btn). Status tabs All/Approved/Pending show live counts and drive the status query param (all is omitted; pending/approved sent as ?status=). A SearchInput sets `search` -> ?search=. Default view: perPage 10, page 1, type table, titleField 'vendor', sort registered desc. Columns: vendor (UserCard avatar+name+email, click -> /vendors/:id, sorting off), phone (or em-dash), registered (DateTimeHtml.Date, the only sortable column -> orderby=registered&order=asc|desc), status (Enabled green / Disabled gray pill). Row actions: Edit (-> /vendors/edit/:id), See Products (full page nav to wp-admin edit.php?post_type=product&author=:id), See Orders (full page nav to adminOrderListUrl&vendor_id=:id), Switch to (only eligible when item.switch_url present AND dokanAdminDashboardSettings.vendors.is_vendor_switching_enabled), Approve Vendors (eligible when !item.enabled, supportsBulk, PUT /dokan/v1/stores/:id/status {status:'active'} or bulk POST /dokan/v1/stores/batch {approved:[ids]}), Disable Selling (eligible when item.enabled, destructive, supportsBulk, PUT .../status {status:'inactive'} or batch {pending:[ids]}). All approve/disable actions route through a DokanModal confirmation with single vs bulk copy; bulk clears selection after success. Loading state shows pulse skeleton (loadingClass). Empty/error path: fetch catch sets data=[] and total=0. Extensibility: applyFilters dokan_admin_vendors_list_filters (filter fields, lite empty), dokan_admin_vendors_before_request (query mutation), dokan-admin-vendors-list-column-fields (columns), dokan-admin-vendors-list-view (view), plus PluginArea scope 'dokan-admin-vendors-list-page'. PRO injects: a 'Store Categories' button (when store_category_type !== 'none'), a 'Categories' column, a store_categories query filter from ?store_categories= search param, and a /store-categories route. Existing automated coverage is route-mount smoke only (stores.spec.ts 'Admin Vendors (React)' TC1-5, admin.spec.ts TC2/TC12); the legacy storesPage helper methods (searchVendor, filterVendors, addVendor, updateVendor, vendorBulkAction, viewVendor) are EMPTY STUBS so the legacy 'Stores test' cases assert nothing.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/stores/stores.spec.ts; /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts`

### Vendor single (detail) — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors/:id` _(mixed)_

VendorsSingle detail view at #/vendors/:id. index.tsx loads the vendor via parseInt(vendorId) and shows SkeletonLoader while (!vendor || isLoading) — a non-numeric or non-existent id therefore renders a permanent skeleton. Layout: HeaderNavigation (Vendors List back-link via window.open _self, Vendor Profile title, Visit Store button opening shop_url in a new tab, Pen icon navigating to /vendors/edit/:id), HeaderCard (Banner image-or-SVG placeholder, StoreAvatar gravatar-or-placeholder, store_name or 'No Name', Rating shown only when vendor.rating exists, StoreCategories showing max 2 + 'N+', Featured warning badge [Pro], Enabled/Disabled badge), left InfoCard (PluginArea scope 'dokan-admin-dashboard-vendors-single'; Registered Since with humanTimeDiff tooltip; Contact phone/email with email truncated to 30 chars + tooltip; Commission Type Fixed/Category Based; Product Publishing Direct Publish[trusted]/Requires Review; active Payment Methods joined; Social Links; many SlotFills), and TabSections (Overview pos0 / General pos1 / Withdraw pos5, filterable via 'dokan-admin-vendor-tabs', default active 'overview'). OverviewTab shows Top Selling Products grid (ProductCard) or NoInformation 'No Top Selling Products' empty state, with skeleton while loading. GeneralTab shows Profile & Address (Name, Country mapped from code, City, State, Address street_1+street_2, Zip; '--' defaults). WithdrawTab GETs dokan/v1/settings?vendor_id=, renders Connected / Not Connected badges and WithdrawPaymentRow per active method (Bank/PayPal/Skrill/Custom) with Manage/Add buttons opening FormModal; MethodAction PUTs dokan/v1/settings and toasts success/error; non-chargeable methods render a Minus. PayPal/Skrill require email, Custom requires input, Bank validates settings-driven required fields. Pro injects 'Send Email' (Fill 'dokan-admin-vendor-after-info-contents', POST /dokan/v1/stores/:id/email, Send disabled until subject+body present) and VendorSalesOverview (Fill 'dokan-admin-vendor-before-top-selling-products', Store + Sales stat cards via getVendorStats). Existing automated coverage is effectively only stores.spec.ts 'Test Case 5 - Vendor detail route /vendors/:id mounts' (route smoke @pro); all legacy StoresPage methods (viewVendorDetails, emailVendor, etc.) are empty stubs and assert nothing.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/stores/stores.spec.ts`

### Vendor create — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors/create` _(mixed)_

The Admin React "Add New Vendor" form at #/vendors/create (Create.tsx) renders a tabbed form (General, Commission, Social Options) inside the dokan-admin-dashboard shell. General tab (form/General.tsx) holds three Cards: Permission (Enable Selling, Publish Product Directly, Make Vendor Featured toggles), Store Information (Store Name + live slug availability, Store Url, Logo/Banner via WpMedia, First/Last name, Email + live availability, Username + live availability, Password + Generate, Phone with input sanitization), and Address Information (Country select -> dynamic State select-or-text, City, Address 1/2, Zip). Commission tab (form/Commission.tsx) has a commission-type SearchableSelect (Fixed / Category Based) revealing FixedCommissionInput or CategoryBasedCommission with a sub-category-inheritance confirm modal. Social Options tab (form/SocialOptions.tsx + SocialField.tsx) renders per-platform URL fields with domain validation (socialValidation.ts). A "Send an email to vendor about account" checkbox (bound to show_email) shows on create. Required fields (email, store_name, user_login, user_pass) come from filter dokan-create-vendor-required-fields and are validated client-side by validateForm (Utils.ts) before POST /dokan/v1/stores/. On success: toast + navigate to /vendors; on error: error toast. The "enabled" default derives from dokanAdminDashboardSettings.vendors.new_seller_enable_selling === 'automatically'. Pro injects via SlotFill scope dokan-admin-dashboard-vendor-form-dokan-create-new-vendor (social card) and filter dokan-create-vendor-initial-data (enable_manual_order). Existing automated coverage is route-mount smoke only: stores.spec.ts "Test Case 3 - /vendors/create form mounts" and admin.spec.ts "Test Case 3 - /vendors/create mounts" just assert the shell + one input mounts; the legacy "admin can add vendor" test calls an EMPTY no-op stub (storesPage.ts addVendor/searchVendor are `{}`), so there is NO real functional coverage of field entry, validation, availability checks, commission, social, or submission.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/stores/stores.spec.ts; /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts`

### Vendor edit — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors/edit/:id` _(mixed)_

The Edit page (src/admin/dashboard/pages/vendor-create-edit/Edit.tsx) renders at hash route #/vendors/edit/:id (route id 'vendor-edit'). On mount it clears form state then GETs dokan/v1/stores/:id; while the vendor object is empty it shows VendorFormSkeleton. It reuses the same Form module as Create with formKey 'dokan-edit-vendor' and createForm=false, so create-only fields (Store URL, Username, Password, send-email checkbox, store-slug availability check) are NOT rendered in edit. Form has 3 tabs: General (Permission toggles enabled/trusted/featured, Store Name [required], Logo + Banner upload/change/remove, First/Last name, Email [required, availability check via dokan/v1/stores/check], sanitized Phone, Country/State/City/Address1/Address2/Zip), Commission (Admin Commission type Fixed -> percentage+additional fee, or Category Based -> inheritance ToggleSwitch with confirm DokanModal + CategoryBasedCommission tree), and Social Options (per-platform URL inputs with domain validation). Required fields are email + store_name (filterable). Save POSTs dokan/v1/stores/:id and toasts 'Vendor Updated Successfully.'; failures toast the error message. Discard is disabled unless the form is dirty and reverts to the loaded vendor. Dirty-state tracking adds a beforeunload handler and a hashchange interceptor that opens a leave-confirm DokanModal ('Are you sure you want to leave?' / 'You have unsaved changes.' / 'Leave Page' / 'Stay', namespace dokan-edit-vendor-unsaved-changes) and reverts the hash to keep the user on the page. NOTE: contrary to the area brief there is NO delete button or delete-confirm modal in Edit.tsx; the only modal is the unsaved-changes leave guard. Pro extends the form via applyFilters('dokan-vendor-form-data-tabs-dokan-edit-vendor') and PluginArea scope 'dokan-admin-dashboard-vendor-form-dokan-edit-vendor' (e.g. extra tabs/sections, featured/category-based commission). EXISTING COVERAGE: stores.spec.ts 'Admin Vendors (React)' has route-mount smokes for #/vendors, /vendors/create and /vendors/:id but NONE for /vendors/edit/:id; the legacy 'admin can edit vendor info' test is test.skip and its editVendor() page-object method is an empty stub. So this edit route currently has ZERO real automated coverage.

### Withdraw (admin) — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/withdraw` _(mixed)_

Admin React WithdrawPage at #/withdraw renders a DataViews list of vendor withdrawal requests with four status tabs (pending/approved/cancelled/all) showing live counts from REST response headers (X-Status-Pending/Completed/Cancelled; note 'approved' maps to backend 'completed'). Default active tab is pending; the menu deep-links to #/withdraw?status=pending. Columns: vendor (gravatar + store_name, truncated at 22 chars with tooltip, rendered via RawHTML), amount, status (color-coded badge), method, charge, payable (receivable||amount), date, details (method-specific via processDetails for bank/paypal/skrill/dokan_custom, truncated), note (truncated). Row + bulk actions: View, Approve (pending-only, has insufficient-balance guard comparing user.balance vs amount), Cancel (pending-only), Add Note (non-approved only), Delete (non-approved only), Download PayPal mass payment file (paypal method only -> /dokan/v1/reports/withdraws/export with status polling). DokanModals: approve/cancel/delete confirmation with singular/plural copy, add-note TextArea, view modal (amount + From header, withdraw details, payment method/note, radio action selector), insufficient-balance warning modal. Filters: Vendor (VendorAsyncSelect), Payment Method (AsyncSelect from /dokan/v2/withdraw/payment_methods), Date Range; supports clear-single-filter and reset-all. Data via GET dokan/v2/withdraw; mutations via POST /dokan/v2/withdraw/batch and PUT /dokan/v2/withdraw/{id}. Export button hits dokan/v2/withdraw?is_export=true. EXISTING COVERAGE: only admin.spec.ts 'Test Case 10 - /withdraw (admin) mounts' (route-mount smoke test, no feature/action assertions). All other withdraw specs (new-withdraw, withdraws) target the VENDOR frontend dashboard/new/#/withdraw and do NOT cover this admin route.

### Reverse withdrawal (admin) — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/reverse-withdrawal` _(mixed)_

The admin Reverse Withdrawal React area has two HashRouter routes: '/reverse-withdrawal' (ReverseWithdrawalPage) and '/reverse-withdrawal/store/:id' (ReverseWithdrawalTransactionPage). The list page renders four stat cards (Total Collected=credit, Remaining Balance=balance, Total Transactions, Total Vendors) populated from REST response headers (X-Status-Credit/Balance/Total-Transactions/Total-Vendors), an "Add New" button opening AddReverseWithdrawModal, and an AdminDataViews table with columns Store / Amount(balance) / Date(last_payment_date). Filters: Vendor async-select + Date Range picker; per-filter and reset clearing. Clicking a store name navigates to the per-store drill-down which fetches dokan/v1/stores/:id (store header) and dokan/v1/reverse-withdrawal/transactions?vendor_id=:id&per_page=-1, rendering columns Transaction ID (external link or '--'), Date, Transaction Type, Note, Debit, Credit, Balance (negatives shown in parentheses), plus a Back button and date-range filter. AddReverseWithdrawModal POSTs to dokan/v1/reverse-withdrawal/transactions with required fields vendor + (product|order depending on type) + amount + note; Transaction Type tabs Product/Order/Other; Debit/Credit toggle; confirm disabled (hasValidationErrors) until valid; Product/Order selects disabled until a vendor is chosen and scoped to that vendor (author/seller_id, post_status=publish). Server permissions: stores-balance and create-transaction require manage_options (admin-only); transactions GET only requires logged-in seller and FORCES vendor_id to current user for non-admins (IDOR guard at ReverseWithdrawalController.php:236-238). Route is always registered in Lite Dashboard but is functionally a Pro feature. EXISTING COVERAGE IS WEAK: admin.spec.ts TC9 and reverseWithdraws React TC1/TC2 are route-mount/empty-or-table smoke tests only; the legacy page-object methods (adminReverseWithdrawRenderProperly, filterReverseWithdraws, clearFilterReverseWithdraws, addReverseWithdrawal) are EMPTY STUBS (no-op bodies in reverseWithdrawsPage.ts:43-46) and two filter tests are test.skip, so the create/filter/drill-down flows have effectively no real assertions.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/reverse-withdraws/reverseWithdraws.spec.ts; /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts`

### Setup guide — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/setup` _(mixed)_

The Setup Guide is a React multi-step onboarding wizard mounted at the HashRouter route '#/setup' inside the admin Dokan dashboard (#dokan-admin-dashboard). Source: src/admin/dashboard/pages/setup-guide/. The wizard is driven by 4 Lite steps registered via AdminSetupGuideServiceProvider in priority order: Basic (priority 10, NOT skippable), Commission (20), Withdraw (30), Appearance (40). No Pro plugin extends the steps (grep of dokan-pro found nothing), so the "vendor steps" in the area summary are not present in current code — only admin-facing steps exist; edition is effectively lite for the wizard itself, but tests run in a mixed environment.

Architecture: index.tsx loads steps from localized getSettings('setup').steps; StepComponent.tsx renders the left vertical stepper (numbered circles -> purple check on completion, with a 1.5s 'animate-complete-step' animation, current step title in purple #7047EB). StepSettings.tsx fetches each step's fields from GET /dokan/v1/admin/setup-guide/{id}, renders SettingsParser -> Section/SubSection/FieldGroup/FieldParser. FieldParser maps variants to inputs: text, select (FormSelect), password, tel, radio_box (SelectorCard with Admin/Vendor icons), radio (segmented buttons), currency (DokanCurrencyInput, rejects negative with 'Invalid value'), switch (ToggleSwitch with enable_state/disable_state), multicheck (SimpleCheckboxGroup), category_based_commission, combine_input (percentage+flat); unknown variants fall back to Text and applyFilters hooks. Navigation: Back (only when previous_step exists), Skip (only when step.skippable — Basic is not skippable), Next (saves via POST /dokan/v1/admin/setup-guide/{id} then advances; label 'Saving…' while in-flight; disabled while saving/loading/erroring). When every step.is_completed the wizard swaps StepSettings for CompletedStep ('Your Marketplace is ready to explore', 'N of N tasks completed', 'Visit Dokan Dashboard' button -> dashboard_url) and POSTs {setup_completed:true} to /dokan/v1/admin/setup-guide/.

States: 30s fetch timeout (Promise.race) -> error screen with 'Failed to load settings' + 'Retry Loading' button; SkeletonLoader during loads (per-step skeleton state); error auto-hides after 5s; smooth scroll-to-top on step change. Dependencies engine (settingsDependencyParser/Applicator) shows/hides fields by comparison rules — e.g. Commission step shows admin_commission combine_input only when commission_type==='fixed' and category_based_commission only when ==='category_based'. Field titles/descriptions render via RawHTML (XSS surface). REST permission_callback = current_user_can('manage_woocommerce') for ALL setup-guide endpoints; invalid step id -> 404 'Invalid step'; save exception -> 400.

Regression note: tests/pw/tests/e2e/_env.setup.ts:124 sets option dokan_admin_setup_guide_steps_completed='1' to dismiss the dashboard banner, but the wizard's CompletedStep is gated on per-step options (dokan_admin_onboarding_setup_step_{id}_completed), not that global option — so the wizard can still show steps after env setup. Existing automated coverage is shallow: setupGuide.spec.ts 'Admin Setup Guide (React)' TC1-3 and admin.spec.ts TC5 only assert route mount, non-blank body, reload survival, and no fatal banner. No automated test exercises any step field, Next/Back/Skip navigation, save persistence, validation, dependencies, the completed screen, or authorization.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/setup-guide/setupGuide.spec.ts; /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts`

### Extensions — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/extensions` _(mixed)_

Extensions page (route '/extensions', id 'extensions', menu hidden:true and appended under Settings, capability-gated by the dashboard page capability). ExtensionsPage renders a marketing banner ("Supercharge Your Marketplace") plus a tabbed UI (@wedevs/plugin-ui Tabs) with 4 tabs: "Picks for you" (RecommendedAddons, defaultValue="recommended"), "Mobile Apps" (MobileApps), "Compatibility" (Compatibility), "Services" (Services). RecommendedAddons is data-driven from getSettings('extensions').extensions.recommended (13 addons defined server-side in Extensions.php, sorted client-side by position via sortByPosition). Each card shows a Free badge (button_type 'install') or Pro/crown badge (button_type 'get_plugin'); install cards POST /dokan/v1/admin/extensions/install with the wp_org_slug and transition Install Free -> Installing… (spinner, disabled) -> Installed (disabled secondary); get_plugin cards render "Get Addon" external links; cards already installed render disabled "Installed". For booking/auction, the installed flag is true when Dokan Pro module is available (Lite vs Pro difference). ExtensionIcon falls back to PlaceholderIcon on broken/empty image. A 3-item ecosystem section (14-day guarantee, 24/7 support, regular releases) renders below, filterable via dokan_extensions_ecosystem_items. MobileApps renders 3 cards (Customer/Vendor/Delivery Driver) with audience badges and "Get App" external links (sorted by position). Compatibility renders 2 static cards (50+ Themes, 100+ Plugins) with external Browse links. Services renders a weLabs card with "Visit weLabs" external link and "View Details" which opens a DokanModal (@wordpress/components Modal: focus trap, ESC, click-outside) listing custom-dev benefits and a "Book a meeting" calendly link. REST install endpoint permission = current_user_can('manage_woocommerce'); returns 400 for unknown slug, 500 on install failure, success if already installed. Existing automated coverage: admin.spec.ts React "Test Case 6 - /extensions mounts" is a route-mount smoke test only (asserts .pui-root visible, URL hash, no fatal). No dedicated extensions spec exists; all functional/tab/install/modal behavior is uncovered.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts`

### Changelog — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/changelog` _(mixed)_

Changelog page (#/changelog, route id 'changelog') renders ChangelogPage: a "Dokan Changelog" title, a "Jump to version" SearchBar dropdown, a Lite/Pro PackageToggle (only when Pro exists), and a vertical list of VersionRow cards with ChangeBadge labels (New/Fix/Improvement/Update). Data comes from REST GET /dokan/v1/admin/changelog/{lite|pro} (permission: manage_woocommerce), parsed from a JSON string, cached client-side per package (no refetch on toggle-back). Latest version (index 0) is always fully expanded and tagged 'Latest' with no toggle; non-latest versions with >5 total change items collapse to the first 5 and show a 'See Details'/'See Less' toggle. SearchBar filters versions by substring, shows latest 5 when empty, shows 'No versions found' on no match, closes on click-outside or selection, and clears via X; selecting a version highlights it (purple ring + full-screen overlay that clears on click) and smooth-scrolls to it. The page is reached via the Header help menu 'Changelog' link. Empty/error states (fetch error is swallowed to console.error) both render 'No changelog data available.'. Existing automated coverage is only admin.spec.ts 'Test Case 7 - /changelog mounts', a route-mount smoke test with a relaxed WP-fatal-banner check (changelog body copy legitimately contains the word 'error').

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts`

### Dummy data importer — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/dummy-data` _(mixed)_

Dummy data importer at #/dummy-data renders one of three body states from index.tsx: StatusSkeleton (while GET dokan/v1/dummy-data/status loads), Importer (when import_status != 'yes': progress bar + 'Run the Importer' button), or Result (when import_status == 'yes': 'Dummy Data Import complete!' heading, 'View Dummy Vendors' button -> navigate('/vendors'), 'View Dummy Products' anchor -> wp-admin edit.php?post_type=product, and a 'Clear Data' danger button that opens a DokanModal confirmation). On mount it also fetches the CSV from window.dokanAdminDashboard.urls.dummy_data and papaparses it into vendor/product rows. Import POSTs dokan/v1/dummy-data/import recursively one vendor at a time (vendor_index advances on each response, progress = 100*succeeded/totalVendors); a success toast fires only when at least one vendor was imported. Clear DELETEs dokan/v1/dummy-data/clear then reloads status+CSV. All three endpoints require current_user_can('manage_woocommerce'). 'Back to Tools' button navigates to '/tools', which exists only in Dokan Pro. Errors surface as DokanToaster toasts ('CSV URL missing', 'Failed to load CSV', 'Something went wrong', import/clear errors). Currently covered only by admin.spec.ts 'Test Case 8 - /dummy-data mounts', a shallow route-mount smoke test with no functional import/clear/state assertions.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts`

- [ ] **[P2·happy]** Loading the #/dummy-data route shows the StatusSkeleton while GET dokan/v1/dummy-data/status is in flight, then resolves to the importer or result body `@lite @admin` — _Throttle/delay the status endpoint and assert the animate-pulse skeleton (.animate-pulse with sr-only 'Loading…') is visible, then disappears once status resolves. Existing TC8 only waits for .pui-root._

Assert progress element value=0, '0%' text, 'Data Importer' heading, 'Normally it takes less than 1 minute.' copy, and the 'Run the Importer' DokanButton is enabled._

Verifies updateProgress math in index.tsx._

### Settings - General (dokan_general) — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan#/settings` _(mixed)_

Settings is NOT in the new React #dokan-admin-dashboard app. It still lives in the LEGACY Vue admin app (src/admin/pages/Settings.vue) mounted at page=dokan#/settings; sections come from includes/Admin/Settings.php get_settings_sections() and fields from get_settings_fields(). The General section (id dokan_general) holds three sub-sections: (1) Site Settings — Admin Area Access (switcher, default on; also force-blocked when HPOS on), Vendor Store URL (text, default 'store', sanitize_custom_store_url rejects reserved slugs like shop/cart/checkout/page and flushes rewrite rules on change), Vendor Setup Wizard Logo (file/media), Vendor Setup Wizard Message (wpeditor), Disable Welcome Wizard (switcher, default off); (2) Vendor Store Settings — Store Terms and Conditions (switcher off), Store Products Per Page (number, default 12), Enable Address Fields (switcher off); (3) Product Page Settings — Enable More Products Tab (switcher on). A 'Data Clear' switcher (data_clear_on_uninstall) is appended via add_dokan_data_clear_setting and shows a Swal confirm before enabling. Save goes through AJAX dokan_save_settings (nonce dokan_admin, cap manage_woocommerce, section whitelist, per-field sanitize); read via dokan_get_setting_values. The page has a debounced (1s) search that filters sections/fields by label+desc, a Documentation link per section, a Back-to-top button, localStorage 'activetab' tab persistence, and a mobile drawer (MobileSettingsList.vue). EXISTING COVERAGE: only three React smoke tests in settings.spec.ts ('Admin Settings (React)') verify the page mounts, shows headings, and survives reload — all page-level, not per-tab. The legacy setting/setting.spec.ts is entirely test.describe.skip and its page objects are empty stubs; the settings/settingsPage.ts general methods (setDokanGeneralSettings etc.) are empty stubs and those tests are test.skip. So functional per-field coverage of the General tab is effectively absent.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/settings/settings.spec.ts ; /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/setting/setting.spec.ts`

Assert settings_title 'General Settings' and the three sub-section labels (Site Settings, Vendor Store Settings, Product Page Settings) are visible._

### Settings - Selling Options — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan#/settings (legacy Vue app, section id dokan_selling)` _(mixed)_

The Selling Options panel (section id `dokan_selling`) lives in the legacy Vue admin settings shell (src/admin/pages/Settings.vue), reached at page=dokan#/settings then clicking the "Selling Options" nav tab. Fields are defined server-side in includes/Admin/Settings.php::get_settings_fields() under the `dokan_selling` key, merged from three groups: (1) Commission — commission_type select (Lite options: fixed, category_based; Pro filter adds flat/percentage/combine via dokan_commission_types), commission_fixed_values composite (admin_percentage Percent Fee + additional_fee Fixed Fee, both required, shown only when type=fixed), reset_sub_category_when_edit_all_category switcher and commission_category_based_values tree (shown only when type=category_based, Pro-flagged dokan_pro_commission); (2) Fee Recipients — shipping_fee_recipient, tax_fee_recipient (Product Tax Fee), shipping_tax_fee_recipient radios each Vendor/Admin (default seller); (3) Vendor Capabilities — new_seller_enable_selling select (Automatically/Manually; default automatically), one_step_product_create switcher, disable_product_popup switcher (shown only when one_step_product_create=off), order_status_change switcher, dokan_any_category_selection switcher. Pro injects more into the same section via dokan_settings_selling_option_vendor_capability: product_status (Published/Pending Review radio), vendor_duplicate_product, product_category_style (Single/Multiple), product_vendors_can_create_tags, add_new_attribute, hide_customer_info, seller_review_manage, plus VendorDiscount, Shipping, and ManualOrders fields. Save flow: Vue saveSettings() POSTs ajax action=dokan_save_settings with nonce=dokan.nonce + section=dokan_selling; server requires manage_woocommerce cap + valid dokan_admin nonce, sanitizes via wc_format_decimal, and clamps admin_percentage to 0-100 (validate_fixed_price_values resets out-of-range to saved value). Client-side formIsValid() blocks save when required fields (admin_percentage, additional_fee, or category_based all.flat/all.percentage) are empty, and selectively clears irrelevant errors based on commission_type. The reset_sub_category_when_edit_all_category toggle triggers a SweetAlert confirmation (Enable/Disable Commission Inheritance) that reverts the switch on cancel. The Vue search box filters sections/fields by label+desc text. Active tab is persisted in localStorage("activetab"). CURRENT COVERAGE: commission.spec.ts has 3 real @lite tests (fixed config save, category-based config save, product-level commission); settings.spec.ts has 3 React-style smoke tests (mount, section-headings regex incl. "selling", reload) plus a skipped no-op setDokanSellingSettings stub and a generic search('Selling Options') test backed by an empty page-object stub. No tests assert fee recipients, vendor capability toggles, conditional field visibility, validation messages, commission clamping, persistence after reload, or authorization.

### Settings - Withdraw Options — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan#/settings (section id dokan_withdraw)` _(mixed)_

The Withdraw Options area is section id `dokan_withdraw` inside the legacy Vue admin settings app (src/admin/pages/Settings.vue), reached at page=dokan#/settings then clicking the "Withdraw Options" nav tab. Fields are server-defined in includes/Admin/Settings.php::get_settings_fields() under the `dokan_withdraw` key. LITE fields: (1) withdraw_methods — multicheck of dokan_withdraw_get_methods() = PayPal + Bank Transfer, default {paypal}; at least the default is seeded on fresh install via set_withdraw_limit_gateways(). (2) withdraw_charges — "charges" custom field (fixed + percentage per chargeable method), gated by show_if withdraw_methods contains-any, and flagged refresh_after_save:true so a successful save triggers window.location.reload(). (3) withdraw_limit — Minimum Withdraw Limit, price text, default 50, sanitize wc_format_decimal; server validation in set_withdraw_limit_value_validation() rejects negative with 400 + inline error "Minimum Withdraw Limit can't be negative value." (4) withdraw_order_status — multicheck Completed/Processing/On-hold, default {wc-completed}. (5) exclude_cod_payment — switcher, default off. PRO adds: Skrill + a custom withdraw method into withdraw_methods/withdraw_charges (via dokan_withdraw_methods filter + CustomWithdrawMethod.php with withdraw_method_name/withdraw_method_type), the Disbursement Schedule sub-section and schedule types (weekly/biweekly/monthly/quarterly) via Withdraw/Manager.php, and hide_withdraw_option. Save flow: Vue saveSettings() POSTs ajax action dokan_save_settings (nonce dokan.nonce=dokan_admin) with section=dokan_withdraw; for this section it computes a diff of removed active methods and, if any removed, shows a SweetAlert "Withdraw Method Changed" asking whether to send a vendor announcement (Save & send / Save only), and similarly a "Disbursement Schedule Updated" SweetAlert when disbursement_schedule changes. Server enforces current_user_can('manage_woocommerce') + nonce on both get/save AJAX, and rejects unknown section names. Tab selection persists in localStorage key "activetab". EXISTING COVERAGE is weak: settings.spec.ts line 28 calls admin.setDokanWithdrawSettings() but that page-object method is an empty no-op stub (settingsPage.ts:57 `async setDokanWithdrawSettings(_d: any): Promise<void> {}`), so it asserts nothing; React smoke tests (TC1-3) only mount/reload #/settings and check for no fatal error; the search test only searches "Selling Options". No real assertion exists for any withdraw field, validation, the reload-after-charges-save, the announcement SweetAlert, or auth/nonce — all marked no or partial accordingly.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/settings/settings.spec.ts`

### Settings - Reverse Withdrawal — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan#/settings (section id dokan_reverse_withdrawal)` _(mixed)_

The 'Reverse Withdrawal' settings section is registered in dokan-lite at includes/Admin/Settings.php::get_settings_sections (title/icon/doc-link/description) and reached in the legacy Vue settings app at page=dokan#/settings by clicking the Reverse Withdrawal nav tab. The section's FIELDS are registered in dokan-lite includes/ReverseWithdrawal/Admin/Settings.php::load_settings_fields (hooked on dokan_settings_fields p21): enabled (switcher, refresh_after_save=true so saving reloads the page), payment_gateways (multicheck, only Cash on Delivery), billing_type (select By Amount Limit/Monthly, default by_amount), reverse_balance_threshold (number min 0 step 0.5 default 150, shown only when billing_type=by_amount), monthly_billing_day (number 1-28 default 1, shown only when billing_type=by_month), due_period/Grace Period (number 0-28 default 7), failed_actions (multicheck: Disable Add to Cart, Hide Withdraw Menu, Make Vendor Status Inactive; default enable_catalog_mode), display_notice (switcher default on), and a Pro-only send_announcement switcher. Server validation in validate_admin_settings (on dokan_before_saving_settings) enforces: billing_type in {by_amount,by_month}; due_period 0-28; at least one failed_action; by_amount requires threshold>0; by_month requires monthly_billing_day 1-28 AND monthly_billing_day+due_period<=28. On successful save create_reverse_withdrawal_base_product creates the RW base product if missing. Save/read go through the dokan_save_settings / dokan_get_setting_values AJAX endpoints which require manage_woocommerce capability + a valid dokan_admin nonce, and validate the section name against the allowed list. IMPORTANT COVERAGE FINDING: the existing settings page object (tests/pw/tests/e2e/settings/settingsPage.ts) is entirely a STUB - every method including setDokanReverseWithdrawSettings is an empty no-op, and the spec passes reverseWithdraw: {} (empty). So 'admin can set Dokan reverse withdraw settings' in settings.spec.ts asserts NOTHING about this section. The only non-stub tests touching this route are the three generic React smoke tests (page mounts / shows headings / survives reload). The reverseWithdraws.spec.ts file covers the SEPARATE transactions list page and vendor RW behavior, not these settings fields. Net: this settings section is effectively uncovered.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/settings/settings.spec.ts ; /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/reverse-withdraws/reverseWithdraws.spec.ts`

### Settings - Page Settings — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan#/settings (Page Settings tab, section id dokan_pages)` _(lite)_

Page Settings is the dokan_pages section of the legacy Vue admin settings app (Settings.vue), reached at admin.php?page=dokan#/settings then selecting the "Page Settings" nav tab (tab persisted in localStorage.activetab). It exposes five WP-page dropdowns built from get_post_type('page') in Settings.php::get_settings_fields(): Dashboard (dashboard), My Orders (my_orders), Store Listing (store_listing), Terms and Conditions Page (reg_tc_page), and Vendor Onboarding (vendor_onboarding). All are type=select with placeholder "Select page", no required flag, no show_if, no refresh_after_save. Save is an AJAX POST to dokan_save_settings (action) with section=dokan_pages, nonce verified against 'dokan_admin', gated by current_user_can('manage_woocommerce'); success shows "Setting has been saved successfully." Reads come from dokan_get_setting_values (same nonce + permission). Existing coverage is effectively zero for real behavior: settingsPage.ts::setPageSettings is a no-op stub and the "admin can set Dokan page settings" test calls it; the React-era tests only smoke-mount the /settings route and check generic headings. No automated test selects a page, saves, asserts persistence, validates the option dropdowns are populated from published pages, or covers authorization/nonce/XSS. dbData.pageSettings already models dashboard/store_listing/my_orders/reg_tc_page IDs, and _env.setup.ts writes reg_tc_page directly via dbUtils, so the option shape is known.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/settings/settings.spec.ts`

### Settings - Appearance — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan#/settings (legacy Vue app, section id dokan_appearance)` _(mixed)_

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/settings/settings.spec.ts, /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/colors/colors.spec.ts, /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/store-appearance/storeAppearance.spec.ts</existingSpec>
<parameter name="summary">The Appearance section (section id dokan_appearance) lives in the LEGACY Vue admin Settings app (src/admin/pages/Settings.vue), not the new React DataViews surface. Reached via wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan then the "Appearance" left-nav tab (the #/settings hash routes to the Vue Settings component). Fields defined in includes/Admin/Settings.php get_settings_fields()['dokan_appearance']: (Lite) vendor_layout_style radio (New UI/Legacy UI, default legacy), vendor_product_editor radio, show_register_as_vendor switcher (My Account role toggle - ties to the my-account hidden role-input memory), store_map switcher, map_api_source radio google_maps/mapbox (refresh_after_save => full page reload on save), gmap_api_key text (secret_text, show_if map_api_source==google_maps), mapbox_access_token text (secret_text, show_if==mapbox), contact_seller switcher, store_header_template radio_image (default/layout1/layout2/layout3), default_store_banner & default_store_profile croppable_image with restore-to-default, store_open_close switcher, enable_theme_store_sidebar switcher, hide_vendor_info multicheck (email/phone/address), disable_dokan_fontawesome switcher. PRO adds store_banner_width/store_banner_height (croppable cropping dims gated by dokan()->is_pro_exists()), Google reCaptcha keys, and Color Scheme Customizer. Save path: AJAX action=dokan_save_settings with nonce + section=dokan_appearance, server requires manage_woocommerce cap and valid dokan_admin nonce; invalid section/nonce/cap throw DokanException. Settings.vue has a 1s-debounced left-nav search, localStorage activetab persistence, per-section Save button (disableSubmit), and secret_text masking.

CURRENT COVERAGE GAP: settings.spec.ts setDokanAppearanceSettings is a no-op STUB (settingsPage.ts line 60) - the appearance admin UI is NOT actually exercised. The 3 React smoke tests only assert the #/settings page mounts without a fatal error and shows some heading. store-appearance/storeAppearance.spec.ts verifies the STOREFRONT EFFECTS of appearance options but sets every value directly via dbUtils.updateOptionValue (DB), never through the admin Appearance UI, and most of its UI-effect tests are test.skip. colors.spec.ts (Pro module) is largely skipped/needs-rewrite. So saving/validating fields through the actual Appearance settings form, secret masking, show_if conditional fields, croppable image restore, refresh_after_save reload, search, and authorization are all uncovered at the UI level.`

### Settings - Privacy Policy — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan#/settings (section id dokan_privacy)` _(lite)_

The Privacy Policy settings live in the legacy Vue settings app (src/admin/pages/Settings.vue) reached at admin.php?page=dokan#/settings, tab id 'dokan_privacy' (title "Privacy Policy"). Defined in includes/Admin/Settings.php get_settings_fields()['dokan_privacy'], the section has EXACTLY three fields: (1) enable_privacy - switcher, default 'on', desc "Enable privacy policy for vendor store contact form"; (2) privacy_page - a select populated from all WP pages (get_post_type('page')) with placeholder "Select page"; (3) privacy_policy - a wpeditor with a default body containing the [dokan_privacy_policy] shortcode placeholder. Unlike every other settings section, dokan_privacy has NO document_link, so the "Documentation" button must be absent on this tab. Saving posts an AJAX request action=dokan_save_settings, section=dokan_privacy, nonce=dokan.nonce; the server (save_settings_value) requires current_user_can('manage_woocommerce'), a valid 'dokan_admin' nonce, a non-empty/whitelisted section, then update_option('dokan_privacy'). On success Vue shows the "Setting has been saved successfully." green notice (#setting-message_updated). The active tab is persisted in localStorage 'activetab', and the search box filters sections/fields by label+desc+section text after a 1s debounce. Frontend coupling: dokan_privacy_policy_text() (includes/functions.php) returns empty UNLESS enable_privacy==='on' AND a privacy_page is selected, and replaces [dokan_privacy_policy] with a link to that page; it renders on the store contact form. COVERAGE STATE: settings.spec.ts:34-37 invokes admin.setDokanPrivacyPolicySettings but the SettingsPage page object (settingsPage.ts) methods are empty stub bodies (no-ops) - no real assertions execute. React Test Cases 1-3 are whole-page route-mount/reload/heading smoke tests, not privacy-specific. privacyPolicy.spec.ts covers storefront enable/disable behavior but its PrivacyPolicyPage methods are also empty stubs. So privacy-policy ADMIN settings have essentially NO real automated coverage today - only shallow smoke and stubbed flows.

### Pro-injected admin dashboard routes (HashRouter route registration via dokan-admin-dashboard-routes filter) — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/{refund,reports,license,store-categories,tools,announcement,subscriptions,verifications,dokan-seller-badge,vendor-support,admin-store-support,abuse-reports,store-reviews,request-for-quote,wholesale-customer,product-qa,product-advertising,product-form-manager}` _(pro)_

Dokan Pro and its modules register ~30 extra routes into Dokan Lite's single admin HashRouter (createHashRouter in dokan-lite/src/admin/dashboard/components/Dashboard.tsx) by pushing route objects onto the wp.hooks filter 'dokan-admin-dashboard-routes'. Each route renders its own admin DataViews/list/detail/form page: /refund (tabs pending|completed|cancelled, search, bulk Refund Manually / Refund via Gateway / Cancel / Delete, cancel+delete DokanModals, X-Status-* count headers), /reports, /license (activate/deactivate/refresh, masked key, activations-remaining + expiry cards), /store-categories (only registered when store_category_type !== 'none'; also injects a categories column+filter into the vendors list), /tools (sections: create pages, regenerate commission, duplicate suborder checker, regenerate variable product author, import dummy data, plus distance_matrix only when table_rate_shipping module active), /announcement(+/create,/:id), /subscriptions (vendor+package async-select filters, cancel modal with immediately|end_of_current_period radios, activate/cancel bulk actions, recurring/trial badges), /verifications, /dokan-seller-badge(+/new,/edit/:id), /vendor-support(+/:id), /admin-store-support(+/:ticketId/:vendorId), /abuse-reports, /store-reviews, /request-for-quote(+6 sub-routes incl /:id edits), /wholesale-customer, /product-qa(+/:id; admin status tabs all|unread|read|unanswered|answered, vendor+product async-select filters, bulk read/unread/delete), /product-advertising, /product-form-manager. The router appends a catch-all path '*' that renders AdminNotFound. KEY ARCHITECTURAL RISKS for this area: (1) routes are registered in domReady() so only present once each Pro bundle's script loads — module-gated routes 404 when the owning module is inactive; (2) deep-linking/reload to any hash route must survive because all share one SPA host page; (3) every route is admin-privileged UI — non-admins (vendor/customer) must never render these admin DataViews; (4) IDOR on the :id / :ticketId/:vendorId detail routes; (5) note path inconsistencies in source — store-reviews uses path:'store-reviews' and element:ReviewList (component reference, not <ReviewList/>), product-advertising uses 'product-advertising' (no leading slash) — both worth a regression render check. EXISTING COVERAGE: license is deep; refund/store-categories/subscriptions(admin) have only thin/legacy or wrong-surface coverage (refund=2 route-mount smokes, store-categories=legacy edit-tags.php only, subscriptions=vendor user-subscription smoke not admin #/subscriptions); reports/announcements/store-support/verifications/wholesale/abuse/product-adv/product-qa/store-reviews/rfq/seller-badges/product-form-manager have functional feature coverage (mostly legacy page-objects). The biggest gaps are the cross-route integration concerns (deep-link/reload, 404 fallback, non-admin authorization, module-gated 404) and the admin #/refund and admin #/subscriptions DataView action flows.

### Global navigation / routing / NoMatch / header / help menu / auth — `wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/*` _(mixed)_

The admin React dashboard is a HashRouter shell (createHashRouter in src/admin/dashboard/components/Dashboard.tsx) mounted in #dokan-admin-dashboard. Each route is wrapped in Layout (SlotFillProvider + ThemeProvider pluginId 'dokan-admin-dashboard' + per-route PluginArea scope 'dokan-admin-dashboard-<id>' + DokanToaster). Routes: '/', '/setup', '/pro-modules', '/withdraw', '/vendors', '/vendors/:id', '/vendors/create', '/vendors/edit/:id', '/reverse-withdrawal', '/reverse-withdrawal/store/:id', '/extensions', '/changelog', '/dummy-data', plus a filter-extensible 'dokan-admin-dashboard-routes' hook and a final catch-all '*' that renders AdminNotFound (src/layout/admin404.tsx): a '404' heading, title \"Sorry, the page can't be found\", message, and a primary \"Back to Dashboard\" button linking to window.dokanAdminDashboard.urls.adminDashboardUrl (legacy admin.php?page=dokan). withRouter() (src/routing/index.tsx) injects navigate/params/searchParams and enforces capabilities via coreStore.hasCap, rendering Forbidden (src/layout/403.tsx, \"Permission Denied\") when unauthorised — but core admin routes are registered with empty capabilities so 403 only fires for capability-gated routes. WP-level gating: Menu.php/Dashboard.php register page=dokan-dashboard and submenu deep-links under the 'manage_woocommerce' capability (dokana_admin_menu_capability). Header.tsx (data-test-id 'dokan-dahboard-header' — note the typo) always shows a \"Lite: <version>\" badge and, only when is_pro_exists, a \"Pro <plan>: <version>\" badge with CrownIcon. The Get Help dropdown (data-test-id 'dokan-dashboard-header-help-menu-container') opens on pointer-enter / closes on pointer-leave, shows a red notification dot, and lists help_menu_items from Dashboard.php: Changelog (active/highlighted when a new version exists, internal #/changelog), Community (FB, external _blank), Documentation (external), FAQ (external), Basic & Fundamental (legacy #/help), Request a Feature (external), Import dummy data (#/dummy-data). External links get target=_blank rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"; titles render via RawHTML (XSS surface). Existing coverage in admin.spec.ts 'Admin Dokan Dashboard (React)' is route-mount smoke tests (TC1-10), NoMatch (TC11), reload (TC12); setupGuide.spec.ts help-menu hover assertions are test.skip'd (effectively uncovered).

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/admin/admin.spec.ts; /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/setup-guide/setupGuide.spec.ts`

- [ ] **[P3·regression]** Per-route PluginArea scope 'dokan-admin-dashboard-<id>' mounts so a registered SlotFill on a route renders, and is absent on other routes `@lite @admin` — _Layout.tsx mounts <PluginArea scope={'dokan-admin-dashboard-' + route.id}/>. Validates the extensibility contract; also relevant to admin404 before/after Slots._
- [ ] **[P3·regression]** Route registered via the 'dokan-admin-dashboard-routes' JS filter mounts and is inserted before the catch-all '*' `@pro @admin` — _Dashboard.tsx applyFilters('dokan-admin-dashboard-routes') then pushes the 404 last. A filter-added route must win over NoMatch; verify ordering._
- [ ] **[P3·edge]** Slow / throttled network shows the PHP 'Loading...' placeholder before React hydrates, then replaces it with the route content (no permanent 'Loading...') `@lite @admin` — _Dashboard.php renders the #dokan-admin-dashboard div with literal 'Loading...'. Under CPU/network throttle confirm hydration completes and placeholder disappears._

Document whether keyboard-only users can open the menu; assert focus visibility and link roles._

Verify heading semantics and link/button accessible names; state must not be conveyed by color alone._

Seed many vendors then time route transitions._

- [ ] **[P3·a11y]** (Dashboard home (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/)) Most Reviewed Products and Most Reported Vendors render side-by-side in a 2-col grid on desktop and stack to 1 col on mobile/tablet `@pro @admin` — _Dashboard.tsx wraps both in grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2; responsive collapse and the case where MostReported is absent (Lite) leaving a single full-width column is untested._

- [ ] **[P2·edge]** (Dashboard home (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/)) Monthly Overview 0->non-zero and non-zero->0 transitions both report exactly 100% change with the correct (inverted for negative metrics) direction `@lite @admin` — _Branch logic in calculatePercentageChange hard-codes 100% for these boundary cases and flips direction for refund/abuse_reports/order_cancellation_rate; both-zero must show neutral 0%._
- [ ] **[P2·regression]** (Dashboard home (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/)) Sales Chart and Monthly Overview each keep an independent MonthPicker; changing one month does not refetch or shift the other section `@lite @admin` — _Each section owns its own monthData state and refetch; cross-contamination between the two pickers would be a real regression._
- [ ] **[P2·negative]** (Dashboard home (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/)) Vendor Metrics section error/empty state renders independently and does not suppress AllTimeStats or TopVendors below it `@pro @admin` — _VendorMetricsSection is Pro-gated and sits between MonthlyOverview and AllTimeStats; a failure there must be isolated per the per-section error pattern._
- [ ] **[P3·a11y]** (Dashboard home (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/)) Sales Chart MonthPicker comparison header and Monthly Overview comparison header localize month/date ranges and render correctly under RTL `@lite @admin` — _comparisonPosition='left' on MonthPicker and date-range labels must mirror in RTL locales; no existing i18n/RTL case for the dashboard date headers._
- [ ] **[P3·a11y]** (Dashboard home (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/)) All section headings and MiniCard/DataView labels render translated strings (dokan-lite/dokan text domains) under a non-English locale `@lite @admin` — _Sections mix 'dokan-lite' and 'dokan' text domains; verify no untranslated/hardcoded strings leak and counters like 'X of Y' localize._
- [ ] **[P2·edge]** (Dashboard home (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/)) Navigating directly to an unknown hash route (e.g. #/does-not-exist) renders the AdminNotFound 404 with a working Back to Dashboard link `@lite @admin` — _Dashboard.tsx registers a path:'*' AdminNotFound route using window.dokanAdminDashboard.urls.adminDashboardUrl; the 404 fallback and its recovery link are untested._
- [ ] **[P3·regression]** (Dashboard home (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/)) Each Layout route mounts only its own PluginArea scope (dokan-admin-dashboard-<route.id>) so Pro fills do not leak across pages `@pro @admin` — _Layout renders PluginArea scope='dokan-admin-dashboard-'+route.id; verifies Pro-registered fills (vendor email/stats) appear only on their intended route and not on the home dashboard._
- [ ] **[P1·regression]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) Switching status tab while a search term is active preserves the search and does not fire two competing /dokan/v1/stores requests (last-wins) `@lite @admin` — _handleTabSelect calls fetchVendors({status,page:1}) imperatively while the useEffect also re-runs on [status]; combined with the [search] dependency this can double-fetch and render stale rows._
- [ ] **[P2·edge]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) Status tab counts (All/Approved/Pending) refresh after an approve/disable action and after a search filter narrows results `@lite @admin` — _counts come from X-Status-* headers set on each fetch; verify the header-driven badge counts re-sync post-mutation and reflect the active search scope, not the global totals._
- [ ] **[P2·edge]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) Bulk Approve on a mixed selection (some already-enabled vendors) only sends still-pending ids and the isEligible filter excludes enabled rows from the action `@lite @admin` — _approve-vendor isEligible=!item.enabled; behavior of a bulk POST when selection includes ineligible vendors (ids in approved[]) must be defined and selection cleared afterward._
- [ ] **[P1·negative]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) A failed approve/disable PUT keeps the confirmation modal loading state reset, surfaces an error, and leaves the row status unchanged `@lite @admin` — _onConfirm has try/finally that resets isConfirmLoading but no catch/toast; a 4xx/5xx on /stores/:id/status must not silently appear to succeed nor leave the modal stuck._
- [ ] **[P3·edge]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) See Products / See Orders full-page nav builds correct URLs from dokanAdminDashboard.urls.adminRoot and adminOrderListUrl with the vendor id appended `@lite @admin` — _see-orders concatenates adminOrderListUrl + '&vendor_id=' assuming the base already has a '?'; verify a well-formed query string and that adminRoot trailing slash yields a valid edit.php URL._
- [ ] **[P2·regression]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) Pro: store_categories slug filter from the URL persists across status-tab changes, pagination, and search via the before_request hook `@pro @admin` — _dokan_admin_vendors_before_request reads searchParams.get('store_categories') on every buildQuery; navigating tabs/pages must keep the category filter applied and reflected in the request._
- [ ] **[P3·edge]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) Pro: Categories column is injected exactly once even when the list re-renders, and shows comma-joined names truncated to 30 chars with full list in tooltip `@pro @admin` — _The add-store-categories-column filter's existing-column find has a buggy no-op predicate (returns undefined), risking duplicate column injection on re-render._
- [ ] **[P1·regression]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) Lite-only: no Categories column, no Store Categories button, no Switch-to action, and no store-categories route are present `@lite @admin` — _All of these are registered only when store_category_type !== 'none' / Pro is active; reverse-gating must be asserted so Lite stays clean._
- [ ] **[P2·regression]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) Switch-to action performs vendor switching and the resulting vendor dashboard is not blocked by the Pro 5.0.0 announcement modal `@pro @admin` — _switch-to sets window.location to vendor.switch_url; combine with closeAnnouncementModal helper guard since the landing page is a vendor /dashboard._
- [ ] **[P2·perf]** (Vendors list (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors)) Search input debounces/coalesces rapid keystrokes so only the final term's /dokan/v1/stores request renders (no stale earlier-result flash) `@lite @admin` — _search is a useEffect dependency firing a fetch per change; without last-request-wins handling fast typing can render results for a stale query._

- [ ] **[P1·security]** (Vendor single (detail) (wp-admin/admin.php?page=dokan-dashboard#/vendors/:id)) Withdraw tab requests dokan/v1/settings scoped to the viewed vendor_id and never returns the admin's own settings `@lite @admin` — _apiFetch sends vendor_id=vendor.id; an IDOR check that the returned connected methods belong to that vendor, plus tampering vendor_id to another store is authorized correctly._

Cross-feature interaction._

## B) Vendor Dashboard — `/dashboard/new/`

### Products list — `/dashboard/new/#/products` _(mixed)_

DataViews-based vendor Products list (Lite src/dashboard/products) gated by capability dokan_view_product_menu and route /products in routes.tsx. Lite renders 7 columns (name+SKU+image, type icon w/ DokanTooltip, stock w/ low-stock<=10 red/green coloring, status DokanBadge, price w/ sale strikethrough, earning, views), status tabs with live counts (All/Published/Pending Review/Draft/In stock/Out of stock), debounced search, 3 filters (Date year_month, Category, Product Type) with reset/remove, row + bulk actions (Edit details, Quick view, View in site, Delete Permanently[bulk], Publish Product/Products[bulk]), a read-only QuickViewModal info table, pagination (X-WP-Total/TotalPages), selection/select-all, subscription product-limit gating that hides the Add-new button and blocks publish, an Add-new-product header button (React editor vs legacy editor when is_legacy_editor_preferred), HashRouter deep-link/reload survival, empty/loading states, and many applyFilters extension points. Data comes from /dokan/v1/products (with include_hidden_products) and /dokan/v1/products/summary; delete=DELETE force, publish=PUT status. Pro (dokan-pro/src/features/products) injects via filters: a Rejected tab + reject status label/badge/count, a Quick Edit action, a Bulk Edit action+modal (DataForm -> /dokan/v3/products/batch), a Duplicate action (/dokan/v2/products/:id/duplicate, gated by can_duplicate_product), a Brand filter (/wp/v2/product_brand, product_brand arg), and the product-adv module adds an Advertise column. Existing spec (newProducts.spec.ts) covers Lite list view, status/stock tabs, search/clear, empty states, add-new + edit navigation, select-all, reload survival, and storefront visibility — but does NOT cover quick view, delete, publish, filters (date/category/type), pagination, sale/earning/low-stock rendering, subscription gating, security/authorization, a11y, or any Pro feature.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-products/newProducts.spec.ts`

- [x] **[P1·happy]** Vendor product list mounts at #/products with DataViews table, seeded row, and no PHP fatal `@lite @vendor` ✅ (newProducts.spec.ts: 'vendor can view product list page (React)' (line 62)) — _Capability dokan_view_product_menu route mount; reactRoot visible, All tab, >=1 row, seeded link, hasNoPhpFatal._

Add Pending Review tab and assert the count badge equals the All count breakdown (All = publish+draft+pending+future)._

Also assert ProductTypeIcon + DokanTooltip label (Simple/Variable/Grouped/External-Affiliate)._

Assert all 8 info rows render and Close button dismisses._

### Product create/edit form (React vendor dashboard) — `/dashboard/new/#/products/create and /dashboard/new/#/products/:productId/edit` _(mixed)_

The product editor is a single React App (src/dashboard/product-editor/App.tsx) mounted for BOTH create (/products/create) and edit (/products/:productId/edit) routes, both gated by the dokan_view_product_menu capability. On mount it fetches the schema-driven form via apiFetch GET /dokan/v3/products/init/fields?id=<id>, shows a Loading skeleton while pending and an InternalError panel with a Refresh button on failure. The Save button is rendered into the page header via a WP Fill slot, is disabled until useFormValidity reports the form is valid, and its label flips between "Save Changes" (new) and "Update Product" (edit). On a successful save it toasts "Product saved successfully." and, for new products only, redirects to window.dokanProductEditor.products_url. An external "Visit Product" link renders only in edit mode. A DokanAI button renders only when ai_settings.ai_text_enable is true. Fields are entirely schema-driven (getFieldConfig + field-config/index handlers): text, slug, Cleave-masked price inputs (regular_price/sale_price) that fetch vendor earning from /dokan/v1/commission, Quill rich-text editors (description/short_description), SKU, GTIN, react-select categories (required), tags, brands (Pro), product-type select, stock status / manage-stock, tax status/class, catalog visibility, shipping class, weight/dimensions, min/max qty, sold-individually, purchase note, enable reviews, single image + gallery (WP media modal), downloadable files, and an Attributes builder. Validation is layered: built-in required + a schema engine (validations/index.ts) supporting required/not_empty/empty and >, >=, <, <= against literals or other fields, with all rules gated by field dependencies/visibility (resolveDependency) so hidden fields are skipped. The Attributes editor (AttributesEdit + AttributeCard) supports custom and global (taxonomy) attributes, drag-reorder, visible/used-for-variations toggles, an Add-New-Term modal gated by window.dokanProductEditor.can_add_new_attribute, Select all/none, and "Default Form Values" selectors for variation attributes. Dokan Pro injects the variations UI via the dokan_product_editor_after_attributes filter (dokan-pro/src/features/product-editor): VariationForm renders only when the product type includes "variable" and at least one attribute is marked for variations (or variations already exist). It offers grouped bulk actions (Add variation, Create variations from all attributes [confirm], Delete all [danger confirm], toggle enabled/downloadable/virtual/manage-stock, set/increase/decrease regular & sale prices [value/percent modals], scheduled sale dates [date modal], stock, shipping dims, download limit/expiry), drag-reorder of variation cards, lazy-loaded per-variation forms (fetchVariationForm on expand) with per-card Save Variation and Remove (window.confirm), and Previous/Next pagination. Note: vendor dashboard tests must dismiss the Pro 5.0.0 announcement modal (closeAnnouncementModal is already wired into the page object constructor).

### Orders list — `/dashboard/new/#/orders` _(mixed)_

The Lite vendor Orders list (src/dashboard/orders/OrderList.tsx) renders a @wedevs/plugin-ui DataViews table fed by useOrders -> GET /dokan/v1/orders (scoped server-side to the current vendor's seller_id; admin can pass seller_id but a vendor is always force-overridden). Columns: order (#number + created date, links to legacy order details via legacyOrdersUrl + viewOrder nonce), order_total (shows strike-through original + net when refunds exist), earning (dash when null), status (DokanBadge by status), customer (billing name, else "Customer"/"Guest"), and a conditional shipment column gated by dokan.allow_shipment==='on' && wc_shipping_enabled && has_shipment_func. Status tabs come from GET /dokan/v1/orders/summary counts; selecting one resets page=1 and clears search. Filters: CustomerFilter (customer_id) and DateRangePicker (after/before, applied on OK, cleared via Reset / per-pill remove). Search is debounced 500ms and cast to absint server-side, so only an exact numeric order id narrows the list (non-numeric is ignored). Row/bulk actions: View, and Change status to on-hold/processing/completed (each isDestructive -> AlertDialog confirm, supportsBulk, isEligible hides the order's current status); confirm fires PUT /dokan/v1/orders/:id then refetches list+counts; multi-result toasts (all-ok / all-fail / partial "N updated, M failed"). Server blocks the PUT when the "Order Status Change" selling option is off. Export dropdown (Fill into dokan-header-actions): Export All / Export Filtered POST a hidden form (with orderExport nonce + current filterArgs) to ordersUrl. Empty state shows ShoppingCart icon + "No Order Yet". When ?order_id= is in the URL the component returns null (legacy single-order takeover). HashRouter route /orders is Lite (cap dokan_view_order_menu); Pro manual-order adds child routes orders/new (CreateOrder auto-creates a draft order then renders the editor) and orders/edit/:id (cap dokan_manage_manual_order). The existing spec covers core view/search/status-tab/row-action/status-change/deep-link happy paths plus customer my-account parity and one cross-role business flow, but omits date-range & customer filters, refund net display, earning/shipment column behavior, bulk actions, partial-failure toasts, export, empty state, pagination, Pro create/edit routes, and all security/a11y/perf cases.

Drives the View/status-change actions against a non-owned :id._

- [ ] **[P3·a11y]** Status is conveyed beyond color (badge text label present, not color-only) so the list is usable without color perception `@lite @vendor` — _DokanBadge renders getStatusLabel text alongside the variant color; assert the textual label is present for each status._
- [ ] **[P3·perf]** Orders list with a large dataset (hundreds of orders) loads within an acceptable time and pagination/search stay responsive `@lite @vendor` 🟡 partial (newOrdersPage.ts waitForListSettled budgets ~15s for the slow polluted-DB list fetch (race handling, not an explicit perf assertion)) — _Seed many orders; assert the initial /dokan/v1/orders fetch + render completes under a threshold and the skeleton clears._
- [ ] **[P2·edge]** Slow-network: while /dokan/v1/orders is in flight the DataViews skeleton rows show and resolve to real rows (no premature empty/zero-count read) `@lite @vendor` 🟡 partial (newOrdersPage.ts waitForListSettled / countRealRows skeleton-subtraction logic (guards the race in the page object, not asserted as a test)) — _Throttle the orders response; assert [data-slot=skeleton] rows render then disappear and the final count excludes skeletons._
- [ ] **[P2·happy]** Pro: vendor with dokan_manage_manual_order sees an 'Add New Order' entry and orders/new auto-creates a draft then renders the order editor `@pro @vendor` — _Pro manual-order index.tsx registers route orders/new (CreateOrder) under parent 'orders'; CreateOrder.tsx calls createOrder() then renders <Order> with the new id, showing EditOrderSkeleton meanwhile._
- [ ] **[P2·happy]** Pro: orders/edit/:id renders the manual-order editor for an existing owned order (cap dokan_manage_manual_order) `@pro @vendor` — _Pro route orders/edit/:id -> UpdateOrder (components/index). Verify editor loads with the order's data._
- [ ] **[P1·security]** Pro: vendor WITHOUT dokan_manage_manual_order cannot access orders/new or orders/edit/:id (route hidden / blocked) `@pro @vendor` — _Both Pro routes gated by capabilities:['dokan_manage_manual_order']. A vendor lacking it must not reach the create/edit editor._
- [ ] **[P1·security]** Pro: orders/edit/:id with a non-existent or another vendor's order id is denied (IDOR on the :id route) `@pro @vendor` — _:id route + REST single-order ownership check; loading a foreign/invalid id should error rather than expose another vendor's order._
- [ ] **[P2·regression]** Lite-only environment exposes no manual-order create/edit routes under orders (no 'Add New Order' menu, orders/new resolves to not-found) `@lite @vendor` — _The orders/new and orders/edit/:id routes are registered only by Pro via the dokan-dashboard-routes filter; in Lite they must not exist._

### Withdraw — `/dashboard/new/#/withdraw (requests: /dashboard/new/#/withdraw-requests)` _(mixed)_

The vendor React Withdraw area spans two HashRouter routes (both gated by cap dokan_view_withdraw_menu). /withdraw mounts three stacked cards: (1) Balance (Your Balance + Minimum Withdraw Amount via PriceHtml, skeleton loader, and a Request Withdraw button gated on window.dokanFrontend.withdraw.isManualWithdrawEnable); (2) PaymentDetails (Last Payment amount/date/method or "You do not have any approved withdraw yet." empty state, a "View Payments" button that navigate()s to /withdraw-requests, a Pro SlotFill "dokan-vendor-dashboard-withdraw-payment-details", and an inline "Pending Requests" DataViews list when pending data exists); (3) PaymentMethods (each active method shows icon/label/info plus an action: disabled "Default", "Make Default" -> POST /dokan/v2/withdraw/make-default-method + success toast, or a "Setup" link to setup_url). The Request Withdraw modal (RequestWithdrawBtn) renders one of four states: a "You already have pending withdraw request(s)" danger alert, a "You don't have sufficient balance" danger alert, a "No payment methods found" warning with a payment-methods link, or the form (Withdraw method SearchableSelect auto-preselecting saved default/first method, Withdraw amount cleave-masked DokanPriceInput with 500ms debounce, live read-only Withdraw charge + Receivable amount from GET /dokan/v1/withdraw/charge). Submit POSTs /dokan/v1/withdraw; amount-required is validated client-side. On /withdraw-requests the button renders into a dokan-header-actions Fill. /withdraw-requests is a DataViews list with status tabs (Pending/Approved/Cancelled with counts from GET /dokan/v2/withdraw/summary), a Date Range filter, pagination, status-dependent columns (Status col on pending, Note col on cancelled; base cols Amount/Method/Date/Charge/Receivable), and a destructive per-row "Cancel" action (pending tab only, confirm "Cancel Withdraw" -> PUT status=cancelled). Pro adds WithdrawSchedule into the slot: renders only when disbursement.enabled, with a toggle, "Edit schedule" button (disabled when toggle off), and a modal of method/preferred-schedule radio/min-balance/reserve-balance selects + next-payment text (POST /dokan/v2/withdraw/disbursement) plus a disable path (POST /dokan/v2/withdraw/disbursement/disable). Existing spec covers Lite happy paths (balance view, tabs, switch tabs, filter controls present, send request, blocked-when-pending, cancel pending, View Payments/Request Withdraw present, HashRouter reload on both routes, admin-approve cross-role flow). Gaps: charge/receivable live calculation, amount-required validation, insufficient-balance alert, no-payment-methods warning, Make Default / Setup payment-method actions, status-dependent columns + Note column, date-range filtering, pagination, cancelled-tab content, Last Payment populated vs empty, security/authorization (cap-less vendor, IDOR/cross-vendor, XSS in note), a11y (modal focus, keyboard tabs), perf (large lists), and all Pro WithdrawSchedule behaviors.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-withdraw/newWithdraw.spec.ts ; /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-withdraw/newWithdrawPage.ts`

### Reverse withdrawal — `/dashboard/new/#/reverse-withdrawal` _(mixed)_

Vendor React Reverse Withdrawal surface (src/dashboard/reverse-withdraw). Route is defined in Dokan LITE routes.tsx (id dokan-reverse-withdrawal, path /reverse-withdrawal, cap dokan_view_withdraw_menu) but the displayed data is populated only when the Pro reverse-withdrawal feature creates a ledger (admin debits for COD commission). The page mounts two parts: (1) ReverseWithdrawalBalance card — shows "Reverse Pay Balance" (parenthesized when negative), then a conditional second line that is "Threshold:" when billing_type==='by_amount' OR "Payable Amount:" when billing_type==='by_month', plus a "Pay Now" primary button rendered ONLY when payable_amount>0. Pay Now opens a DokanModal ("Reverse Withdrawal Payment" / "Are you sure you want to pay this amount?" / formatted_payable_amount / Pay Now / Cancel); confirming calls useAddToCart (admin-ajax dokan_reverse_withdrawal_payment_to_cart with _reverse_withdrawal_nonce) then redirects to window.dokan.reverse_withdrawal.checkout_url; failures (missing nonce, non-success, non-JSON) surface a DokanToaster error. (2) ReverseWithdrawalTransactions — a DataViews table (namespace dokan-reverse-withdrawal-transactions) with non-sortable columns Transaction ID (links to trn_url in a new tab when present), Date, Transaction Type, Note, Debit, Credit (both show '--' when empty), Balance (parenthesized when negative); a Date Range filter (DateRangePicker, onOk sends trn_date[from]=Y-m-d 00:00:00 / trn_date[to]=Y-m-d 23:59:59 to /dokan/v1/reverse-withdrawal/transactions, page reset to 1), Reset/onReset re-fetches unfiltered, pagination via onChangeView re-fetches while preserving lastPayload (date filter persists across pages), and a footer running "Balance:" (summaryBalance from X-Status-Balance header) shown only when rows exist. Data hooks: useVendorDueStatus (GET vendor-due-status, skeleton Loader while loading, renders null if no data), useReverseWithdrawalTransactions (X-WP-Total / X-WP-TotalPages / X-Status-Balance headers), useAddToCart. Existing spec (newReverseWithdraw.spec.ts) covers: route mount + no-fatal, balance/threshold widget visible, all 7 columns present, rows-or-empty-state, filter funnel affordance present, Reset is a no-op, HashRouter reload survives, customer cannot see widget, admin REST parity. NOT covered: Pay Now button visibility gate, the entire DokanModal pay confirmation flow, add-to-cart success redirect + all error toasts, by_month vs by_amount conditional line, negative-balance parenthesization, applying a real Date Range filter and asserting filtered rows, pagination + date-filter persistence across pages, footer running balance value, transaction-ID external link, '--' debit/credit placeholder, loading skeleton, capability gating for a vendor lacking dokan_view_withdraw_menu, and XSS in the note/trn_id columns.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-reverse-withdraw/newReverseWithdraw.spec.ts`
