### Coupons — `/dashboard/new/#/coupons (list); /coupons/create; /coupons/update/:id` _(pro)_

Pro injects the vendor Coupons area into the new React dashboard via wp.hooks 'dokan-dashboard-routes': a list route (/coupons, cap dokan_view_coupon_menu), a create route (/coupons/create, cap dokan_add_coupon), and an edit route (/coupons/update/:id, cap dokan_edit_coupon). An 'Add New Coupon' header button is rendered into the 'dokan-header-actions' Fill slot but only when usePermission('dokan_add_coupon') is true. CouponList is a DataViews surface with two tabs ('My Coupons' -> GET /dokan/v1/coupons with per_page/page/search and X-WP-Total pagination; 'Marketplace Coupons' -> GET /dokan/v1/coupons/marketplace, no pagination, no row actions). Vendor columns: Coupon Code (clickable link only if dokan_edit_coupon, truncated to 25 chars), Amount (percent shows N% else PriceHtml), Discount Type (couponTypeMapping incl. an extra display-only 'Fixed cart discount'), Usage (usage_count/usage_limit||infinity), Expiry Date. Marketplace adds an 'Amount Deduct From'/admin-shared column. Row 3-dot actions Edit (gated by dokan_edit_coupon) and Delete (gated by dokan_delete_coupon, destructive confirm dialog -> DELETE /dokan/v1/coupons/:id). CouponFormWrapper loads useCouponDetails(:id) showing a skeleton while loading and a NotFound 'Coupon not found' when an id 404s; create uses null id. The shared CouponForm fields: code (native required title), description, discount_type SimpleSelect from window.dokanCoupon.coupon_types (percent + fixed_product, no fixed_cart creatable), coupon_amount (percent=number min 0/1 max 100, fixed_product=masked DokanPriceInput), email_restrictions, usage_limit/usage_limit_per_user (number min 1), date_expires (WpDatePicker, no past dates), exclude_sale_items, minimumAmount (priceInput), product & exclude_product_ids (AsyncSearchableSelect, >=3 chars, mutually exclude), categories & exclude_product_categories (SearchableTaxonomy, mutually exclude), show_on_store, free_shipping. Client validation in useCouponForm: required title; amount>0 unless free_shipping; percent<=100; numeric fields valid & non-negative; fixed_product/recurring requires a product OR category; on failure a 'Validation Error' toast + scroll to .has-error; on success a 'Coupon created/updated successfully' toast then navigate('/coupons'). Server param errors are mapped back into field errors.

### Manual order (create/edit) — `/dashboard/new/#/orders/new (edit: /dashboard/new/#/orders/edit/:id)` _(pro)_

Pro registers two React routes under the orders parent — `orders/new` (CreateOrder) and `orders/edit/:id` (UpdateOrder, the `Order`/index component) — both gated by the `dokan_manage_manual_order` capability, which is granted to seller/administrator/shop_manager when the admin setting Selling > Allow Vendors to Create Orders (`dokan_selling['allow_vendor_create_manual_order']`) is saved 'on'. CreateOrder auto-creates a draft order (`created_via: dokan-rest-api`) on mount, shows EditOrderSkeleton while creating, then renders the shared edit form for the new id. The edit form (Order/index.tsx) fetches the order by id and enforces: 404 NotFound view, 403 Forbidden view, and an ownership/capability guard — the order must be created by a vendor (`meta _wc_order_attribution_source_type === 'vendor'`) AND the user must have `dokandar` + `dokan_manage_order`, otherwise a Forbidden view is shown. The form has: a General card (Created Date with date[min=today]/hour 0-23/minute 0-59, Order Status searchable select defaulting to Pending payment, async Customer select debounced 1000ms requiring 3+ chars sourced from /dokan/v1/customers, hidden when `hide_customer_info`); Billing & Shipping Address cards each opening an AddressModal (country/state selects with state falling back to free-text input when no states, Load address, Copy from billing for shipping only, customer note for shipping only, fields readOnly when order is not editable); an Order Items list (Add item(s) -> Add product(s)/Add fee/Add shipping[gated by `shipping_enabled`], Apply coupon[gated by `coupon_enabled`], product search via /dokan/v2/products needing >2 chars with quantity 1-9999, fee name required + amount + tax status, line-item edit modal, "This order is not editable" message when `is_editable` false); an Order Summary (subtotal/discount/shipping/tax/total + applied coupons with remove); Order Notes (add private/customer note with trim validation, delete-with-confirm modal, empty state "No notes found"); and Order actions (action select + Apply with confirm modal, permanent Delete with confirm modal, Save). A BackButton ("← Back") is rendered into the dokan-header-actions Fill slot. The existing spec covers the route gating (enable/disable via wp-cli), happy-path view/create/add-product/status/note, and two negative search cases — but does NOT cover the edit route (orders/edit/:id), addresses, fees, shipping, coupons, totals math, delete, order actions, note deletion, the not-editable lock, IDOR/ownership security, the 404/403 views, the back button, or any a11y/edge/perf cases.

### Shipping (zones list + single zone + shipping policy) — `/dashboard/new/#/settings/shipping` _(pro)_

Pro-only vendor Shipping area registered under the Settings parent ONLY when WooShipping is enabled (dokanShippingHelper.enable_woo_shipping) and the user holds the dokan_view_store_shipping_menu capability. Three React routes via the dokan-dashboard-routes filter: (1) zones list /settings/shipping — a DataViews table (search disabled) with columns Zone Name (a DokanLink that navigates to the zone editor), Region(s) (formatted_zone_location), and Shipping Methods (enabled methods plain, disabled methods rendered struck-through via RawHTML, or "No method found"); a row Edit action; truncation+tooltip on names/methods; ZoneListSkeleton while /dokan/v1/shipping loads. A Fill-slotted header button "Click here to add Shipping Policies" navigates to the policy route, and ShippingHeader optionally shows a legacy-shipping link when enable_dokan_shipping is set. (2) Single zone /settings/shipping/:zoneID — shows Zone Name and Location, then CASCADING location selectors driven by zone_locations: Select Countries appears only when the zone is continent-scoped (isCountrySelectable), Select States only when state-selectable and a country exists, and a "Restrict to Specific ZIP/Postal Code" toggle + comma-separated ZIP input + "Save Changes" only when post-code-selectable. A 404 from /dokan/v1/shipping/:zoneID renders NotFound; SingleZoneSkeleton while loading; errors surface as toasts. Below sits ShippingMethods: a DataViews of methods (Method Title, Status ToggleSwitch with OPTIMISTIC update + rollback on API failure, Description tooltip/truncate), per-row Edit and Delete (delete fires DELETE immediately with NO confirm dialog), and an "Add Shipping Method" button. AddShippingMethod is a DokanModal with a searchable Select of available_methods whose "Add Method" button is disabled until a method is chosen (POST creates with cost 0 + default title). EditShippingMethod is a DokanModal whose fields are conditional on method id: flat_rate shows Cost (+helpText), Tax Status (only if is_tax_status==='yes'), Description, per-shipping-class costs, "No shipping class cost", and Calculation type; free_shipping hides Cost and shows "Free Shipping Requires" plus (when not coupon/none) a Minimum order amount and an "Apply minimum order rule before coupon discount" switch; local_pickup uses cost helpText. (3) Shipping Policy /settings/shipping/settings — Processing Time SearchableSelect (options from dokanShippingHelper.processing_time), Shipping Policy + Refund Policy textareas, all with help tooltips, and a "Save Settings" button persisting to /dokan/v1/shipping/policy. EXISTING COVERAGE: the newShipping spec covers vendor list view, seeded US zone row, opening a zone (link + row action), add flat-rate/free shipping, edit method, delete method, open policy section, reload persistence, business-flow persistence, and admin/customer no-PHP-fatal smoke checks. GAPS this set adds: WooShipping-disabled NotFound gating, capability/security/IDOR on :zoneID, deep-link/back-forward hash nav, location-selector cascade logic, ZIP toggle/save, status-toggle optimistic rollback, add-method modal validation (disabled button + searchable select + cancel), conditional Edit-modal fields per method type, struck-through disabled-method rendering, "No method found", policy save round-trip + field persistence, XSS in policy/description/title, a11y/focus, and large-dataset perf.

### Store SEO settings — `/dashboard/new/#/settings/seo` _(pro)_

Pro-only vendor Settings sub-route (capability dokan_view_store_seo_menu) registered via the dokan-dashboard-routes filter at /settings/seo under the settings parent. StoreSeoForm.tsx renders a single form fetching from GET /dokan/v2/settings/store_seo on mount (loading skeleton, error toast on fetch failure) and POSTing { items } on submit (success/error toast; Save button disabled while isSaving). Fields: SEO Title (meta_title), Meta Description (textarea), Meta Keywords, Facebook Title (og_title), Facebook Description (textarea), Facebook Image (og_image), X/Twitter Title (twitter_title), X/Twitter Description (textarea), X/Twitter Image (twitter_image). Three fields (title/desc/keywords) carry CircleHelp tooltips. ImagePreview.tsx shows a MediaUploader "Upload" button when no image, or a 144px preview with a hover-revealed role=button/tabIndex=0 black overlay + red X to delete. The whole route only exists when admin option store_seo (dokan_general) is 'on'; the storefront <head> meta (document_title_parts title, print_tags description/keywords, print_social_tags og/twitter) only renders on store pages when that option is on. Backend save (SettingsApi/StoreSeo update_store_seo_settings) persists to user_meta dokan_profile_settings['store_seo']; capability re-checked server-side. Existing spec covers the happy path well (view, save all fields, persist-after-reload, single-field update, clear field, cross-role storefront <head> business flow) but the page object's storefront helper for og_image/twitter_image, keywords-in-head, image upload/remove, capability/authorization, error/loading states, tooltips, a11y, and XSS injection are uncovered.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-store-seo/newStoreSeo.spec.ts, /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-store-seo/newStoreSeoPage.ts`

Could be extended to also assert ogDesc, twitterDesc and the two Upload buttons (FB/X image)._

### Social profiles settings — `/dashboard/new/#/settings/social` _(pro)_

Pro-only React social profiles form at /dashboard/new/#/settings/social, registered as a child route under the Settings parent and gated by the dokan_view_store_social_menu capability. The component (SocialProfile.tsx) renders 9 URL inputs driven by the server-localized window.dokanSocialProfile.fields config (fb/Facebook, twitter/X, pinterest, linkedin, youtube, tiktok, instagram, flickr, threads), each with a leading brand icon and id `#social-<key>`. On mount it GETs /dokan/v1/stores/{currentUser.id} (showing SocialProfileSkeleton while isLoading) and on Submit POSTs { social: socialProfile } to the same endpoint. Per-network regex validation runs client-side: empty fields are skipped, non-empty invalid fields show an inline "Invalid {Name} URL. Example: {url}" error, and submitting any invalid value shows an error toast "Please enter valid social profile URLs" and aborts the POST. Successful save shows "Social profile updated successfully"; a failed POST shows "Error updating social profile". During submit all inputs and the Submit button are disabled (isSubmitting). Notable regex quirks: youtube REQUIRES an @handle, twitter accepts x.com, fb accepts fb.com. Three extensibility Slots (before form, form bottom, after form) and a VisitStore-wrapped title exist. The existing spec covers view, save+persist, reload persistence, clear-a-field, and a vendor->guest storefront flow, but only exercises 5 of 9 fields and has no validation, error-toast, disabled-state, skeleton, capability/authorization, IDOR, XSS, or a11y coverage.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-social/newSocial.spec.ts`

### Store reviews — `/dashboard/new/#/reviews` _(pro)_

Pro-only vendor Reviews DataViews list at /dashboard/new/#/reviews, registered via the dokan-dashboard-routes filter and gated by the dokan_view_review_menu capability (index.tsx). It renders product-review comments left on the current vendor's published products (Pro ReviewsController::get_reviews, vendor-scoped via dokan_get_current_user_id) — NOT the dokan_store_reviews post type. UI: a DataViews table (search disabled) with four status tabs — Approved / Pending(hold->moderated) / Spam / Trash — whose counts come from /dokan/v1/reviews/summary comment_counts. Three non-sortable, non-hideable columns: Author (name + email linking to the comment, opens new tab), Review (content truncated to 20 chars via ShortContent + localized date), Rating (5-star fill, gray for empty). perPage=10, pagination = ceil(total/perPage). Row selection drives status-dependent bulk actions (STATUS_ACTIONS_MAP): Approved->Unapprove/Spam/Trash; Pending->Approve/Spam/Trash; Spam->Not Spam/Trash; Trash->Restore/Delete Permanently(destructive). Actions render ONLY when window.dokanReviewsSettings.vendor_review_manage === 'on' (admin setting seller_review_manage in dokan_selling, default on); otherwise the actions array is empty and the list is read-only. Backend status changes call /dokan/v1/reviews/batch-update; permanent delete calls /dokan/v1/reviews/batch-delete and only succeeds on already-trashed reviews. manage_reviews_permission_check requires dokan_manage_reviews cap AND seller_review_manage==='on'; invalid status returns 400. Existing spec covers route mount, four tabs, seeded-review visibility, tab switching, empty state, and reload; it does NOT cover any moderation action through the React UI (status changes, bulk actions, permanent delete, the read-only/actions-hidden setting), pagination, rating-star/date/truncation rendering, author-link, capability/authorization, a11y, or XSS in review content.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-store-reviews/newStoreReviews.spec.ts`

### Seller badge — `/dashboard/new/#/seller-badge` _(pro)_

Pro Seller Badge vendor React dashboard at /dashboard/new/#/seller-badge (route id dokan-seller-badge, cap dokan_view_badge_menu). BadgeList renders a DataViews table with three status tabs (All / My Badges / Available Badges) each carrying a live count, an intro paragraph, a debounced name-only search, two columns (Acquired Badge logo+name+level+check-circle icon, Description), and a per-row "View" action. All filtering, sorting (by vendor_count DESC), search (case-insensitive, badge_name only) and pagination (perPage=15) are CLIENT-SIDE against a single `per_page=-1` fetch of /dokan/v1/seller-badge?is_frontend=true. My Badges = vendor_count>0; Available Badges = vendor_count===0. Switching a tab resets page=1 AND clears the search. The View action opens BadgeDetailsModal which fetches /dokan/v1/seller-badge/{id}?is_frontend=true (spinner while loading, achieved-levels list with condition text via dangerouslySetInnerHTML + acquisition dates, "No levels found" fallback, error toast+close on failure). On page load an UnseenBadgesModal auto-appears if the vendor has freshly-unlocked badges ("Congratulations! You have unlocked N New Badge(s)") and PUTs set-badge-as-seen on close. Check-circle is green (#139f84) when all levels acquired or event is years_active, gray otherwise; level text shows "Level: N of M" (or "Level: N" for years_active). REST: vendor list/detail endpoints require a logged-in seller and is_frontend scopes results to the current vendor (IDOR guard); menu only appears when dokan_is_seller_enabled. EXISTING SPEC covers tab render/switch, intro, search presence, reload, no-match search, and an admin-REST-seed->vendor-sees-available cross-role flow, but ONLY at smoke depth (no PHP fatal / tab counts). It does NOT cover the View details modal, the UnseenBadges modal, level/condition text rendering, check-circle state, My-Badges filter correctness, pagination, search-name-match correctness, disabled-seller gating, capability/authorization, IDOR, or XSS/a11y.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/new-seller-badge/newSellerBadge.spec.ts`

### Announcements — `/dashboard/new/#/announcement` _(pro)_

The Pro vendor React Announcements area has two surfaces. (1) AnnouncementList at the top-level HashRouter route #/announcement (registered via the dokan-dashboard-routes filter, gated by capability dokan_view_announcement). It renders a two-pane master/detail layout: an AdminTab row with All/Unread/Read tabs each showing live counts (fetched via three parallel HEAD-like X-WP-Total requests), a 500ms DebouncedInput search, a funnel button that toggles an AdminFilter date-preset SearchableSelect (presets minus 'custom'), a Select-All checkbox header that swaps to a bulk-action bar (Mark as Read POST / Mark as Unread POST / Delete via DELETE with a DokanModal confirmation) when rows are checked, a per-row checkbox + clickable title region, an unread purple dot, a loading skeleton, a NoAnnouncementFound empty state, and a custom Pagination shown only when total > perPage. Clicking a row opens AnnouncementDetails (RawHTML content in an h2/article) and auto-marks an unread item read (updateReadStatus -> POST /dokan/v1/announcement/notice/:id then refetches counts). List text strips HTML; detail and modal render RawHTML. (2) AnnouncementModal mounted on #dokan-announcement-modal-root only when window.dokanAnnouncementModal.latestUnread exists and latestUnread.id !== lastDismissedId; it shows the latest unread title/content (RawHTML), an unread-count footer with singular/plural noun, a 'View All' link that dismisses then navigates to announcementUrl, dismiss POSTs /dokan/v1/announcement/dismiss-modal; the modal is non-click-outside-closable but Esc- and X-closable. Existing specs cover route mount, reload/hash persistence, modal auto-dismiss coexistence, search narrow/empty/clear, Unread-tab request, detail h2, and customer negative access; gaps remain across bulk actions, mark read/unread/delete confirm flow, auto-mark-read, date filter, pagination, counts, empty/loading states, a11y, and modal lifecycle.

Existing spec: `/Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/announcements/announcementsNewUI.spec.ts, /Users/wedevs/Sites/dokanautomation/wp-content/plugins/dokan-lite/tests/pw/tests/e2e/announcements/announcements.spec.ts`
